EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

1      Introduction

1.1  Overview

This document introduces the supported AT command set of Ec716/718 platform product.

1.2  References

[1] 3GPP TS 27.007 V3.13.0 (2003-03)
[2] ETSI TS 27.005 V3.1.0 (2000-01)
[3] ITU-T V.25 ter (07/1997)

                  LYNQ 

     CONFIDENTIAL


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    24
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

2      V.25ter          AT      Commands

2.1  ATE   - Set echo

This command  determines whether or not the DCE echoes characters received from the DTE during

command  state and online command state.

2.1.1  Format

 Command                 Response

 ATE[<value>]            OK
 Max response time       300 ms

2.1.2  Field

 Parameters       LYNQDescription            
                         0: DCE does not echo characters during command state and online
                         command  state.
 <value>                 1: DCE echoes characters during command state and online command
                         state.
                         The default value is 1.

2.2  ATI  - Request    identification  information

3      Network             service           related          command

3.1  AT+COPS      - Operator   selection

The set command forces an attempt to select and register the network operator using the USIM card

installed in the currently selected card slot. <mode> is used to select whether the selection is done

automatically by the MT or is forced by this command to operator <oper> (it shall be given in format

<format>) to a certain access technology, indicated in <AcT>. If the selected operator is not available,

no other operator shall be selected (except <mode>=4). If the selected access technology is not

available, then the same operator shall be selected in other access technology. The selected

operator name format shall also apply to further read commands (AT+COPS?). <mode>=2 forces an

attempt to deregister from the network. The selected mode affects all further network registration (e.g.

after <mode>=2, MT shall be unregistered until <mode>=0 or 1 is selected). This command should be
abortable when registration/deregistrationLYNQattempt is made.
The read command  returns the current mode, the currently selected operator and the current access

technology. If no operator is selected, <format>, <oper> and <AcT> are omitted.

The test command returns a set of five parameters, each representing an operator present in the

network. A set consists of an integer indicating the availability of the operator <stat>, long and short

alphanumeric format of the operator’s name, numeric format representation of the operator and

access technology. Any of the formats may be unavailable and should then be an empty field. The list
of operatorsCONFIDENTIALshall be in order: home network, networks referenced in USIM or active application in the
UICC  (GSM or USIM) in the following order: HPLMN selector, user controlled PLMN selector,

operator controlled PLMN selector and PLMN selector (in the USIM or GSM application), and other

networks. After the operator list MT returns lists of supported <mode>s and <format>s. These lists

shall be delimited from the operator list by two commas.


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    35
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

 Command                 Response

                         OK
 AT+COPS=<mode>[,<for
                         or
 mat>[,<oper>[,<Act>]]]

                         +CME  ERROR:  <err>
                         +COPS: [list of supported (<stat>, long alphanumeric <oper>, short
                         alphanumeric <oper>,numeric <oper>[,<Act>])s],,(list of supported
                         <mode>s),(list of supported <format>s)
 AT+COPS=?
                         OK
                         or
                         +CME  ERROR:  <err>
 Max response time       60s, depend on network status

                         mode (<mode> = 0) is entered.
                         0: Long format alphanumeric <oper>; can be up to 16 characters long.
 <format>                1: Short format alphanumeric <oper>.

                         String type

     CONFIDENTIALalphanumeric            format can be up to 16 characters long and short
 <oper>                     format up to 8 characters; numeric format is the GSM location area

3.2  AT+CLCK      - Facility lock

This command  is used to lock, unlock or interrogate a ME or a network facility <fac>. Password is

normally needed to do such actions. When querying the status of a network service (<mode>=2) the

response line for ‘not active’ case (<status>=0) should be returned only if service is not active for any

class. This command should be abortable when network facilities are set or interrogated.

3.8  AT+ECPING       - Test IP network    connectivity    to a remote    host

This command  sends an ICMP packet to the specified host address. AT+ECPING initiates the
sending of a PING packet with payload size: <size> to the specified address. This will either cause a
packet to be returned if the remote system is connected and responding to PING packets or no
response will be received. If none of the response packet received within the timeout period
<timeout>.CONFIDENTIALIt will continue to send PING packet until the <count> number of times.
3.8.1  Format

 Command                 Response

 AT+ECPING=[<ipaddr/Url  OK
 >/<mode>[,<count>[,<siz If there is any error, response:
 e>[,<timeout>]]]]       SOCKET  ERROR:  <err>

                         OK

                         a) When one PING reply received in <timeout>, an unsolicited result
                         code: +ECPING: SUCC, dest:<dest_ip_addr>, RTT: <rtt_time>ms will
                         sent to TE.
                         b) If no PING reply received in <timeout>, an unsolicited result code:
                         +ECPING: FAIL, dest:<dest_ip_addr>, time out: <timeout>ms will sent
                         to TE.

                         result code: +ECPING: ERROR, cause: <cause> will sent to TE.

4.1  AT+CFUN      - Set module    functionality

Set command  selects the level of functionality in the MT. Level "full functionality" is where the highest

level of power is drawn. "Minimum functionality" is where minimum power is drawn.

4.1.1  Format

 Command                 Response

                         OK
 AT+CFUN=<fun>[,<rst>]   or
                         +CME  ERROR:  <err>
                         +CFUN: (list of supported <fun>s), (list of supported <rst>s)
 AT+CFUN=?
                         OK
                         +CFUN: <fun>
 AT+CFUN?
                  LYNQOK                     
                         Note: Settings 2 and 3 of <fun> are not supported.
                         <fun> = 0 performs all system shutdown actions without removing
                         power.
                         <fun> = 1 performs a system startup.
                         <rst> = 0 shall always be the default if the <rst> parameter is not given.
 Max response time       60s, depend on network status

4.1.2  Field

 ParametersCONFIDENTIALDescription
                         0: Minimum functionality.
 <fun>                   1: Full functionality (Default).
                         4: Disable phone both transmit and receive RF circuits.
                         0: Do not reset the MT before setting it to <fun> power level. This shall
                         always be defaulted when <rst> is not given.
 <rst>
                         1: Reset the MT before setting it to <fun> power level. (not supported
                         and will be ignored)

4.2  AT+CPIN     - Enter  PIN

This command  sends to the ME a password which is necessary before it can be operated (SIM PIN,

SIM PUK, etc.). If the PIN is to be entered twice, the TA shall automatically repeat the PIN. If no PIN

request is pending, no action is taken towards ME and an error message, +CME ERROR, is returned

to TE.

If the PIN required is SIM PUK or SIM PUK2, the second pin is required. This second pin, <newpin>,

is used to replace the old pin in the SIM.

4.2.1  Format

 Command                 Response

                         OK
 AT+CPIN=<pin>[,<new
                         or

                         +CPIN: <code>

 Max response time       300 ms

 <newpin>                String type values.
                         READY: No further entry needed.
 <code>                  SIM PIN: ME is waiting for SIM PIN.
     CONFIDENTIALSIM         PUK: ME is waiting for SIM PUK.

4.4  AT+CGDCONT         - Define  PDP   context

The set command specifies PDP context parameter values for a PDP context identified by the (local)

context identification parameter, <cid>. It also allows the TE to specify whether security protected

transmission of ESM information is requested, because the PCO can include information that

requires ciphering. There can be other reasons for the UE to use security protected transmission of
ESM  information,CONFIDENTIALe.g. if the UE needs to transfer an APN. The number of PDP contexts that may be
in a defined state at the same time is given by the range returned by the test command.

For EPS the PDN connection and its associated EPS default bearer is identified herewith. For EPS

the <PDP_addr> shall be omitted.

A special form of the set command, +CGDCONT=<cid> causes the values for context number <cid>


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    46
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

to become undefined.

The read command  returns the current settings for each defined context.

The test command returns values supported as a compound value.

4.4.1  Format

 Command                 Response

 AT+CGDCONT=<cid>[,<
 PDP_type>[,<APN>[,<PD
 P_addr>[,<d_comp>[,<h_
 comp>[,<IPv4AddrAlloc>[
                         OK
 ,<request_type>[,<PCSC
                         or
 F_discovery>[,<IM_CN_S

  4.5  AT+CGATT       - PS attach   or detach

  The execution command is used to attach the MT to, or detach the MT from, the packet domain

  service. If the MT is already in the requested state, the command is ignored and the OK response is

  returned. If the requested state cannot be achieved, +CME E RROR response is returned. Any active

  PDP  contexts will be automatically deactivated when the attachment state changes to detached.

  4.5.1  Format
   Command          LYNQResponse               
                           OK
   AT+CGATT=<state>        or
                           ERROR
                           +CGATT: <state>
   AT+CGATT?
                           OK
                           +CGATT: (list of supported <state>s)
   AT+CGATT=?
                           OK
Max response   time        45s, depend on network status
  4.5.2CONFIDENTIALField
   Parameters              Description

                           Indicates the state of PS attachment
   <state>                 0: Detached
                           1: Attached


  Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    49
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

4.6  AT+CGACT       - PDP   context   activate  or deactivate

The set command is used to activate or deactivate the specified PDP context. If any PDP context is

already in the requested state, the state for that context remains unchanged. If the requested state for

any specified context cannot be achieved, a +CME ERROR response is returned. If the MT is not PS

attached when the activation form of the command is executed, the MT first performs a PS attach and

then attempts to activate the specified contexts. If the attach fail, then the MT responds with +CME

ERROR.

For EPS, if an attempt is made to disconnect the last PDN connection, then the MT responds with a

+CME  ERROR.

For EPS, the activation request for an EPS bearer resource will be answered by the network by either

an EPS dedicated bearer activation or EPS bearer modification request. The request must be

accepted by the MT before the PDP context can be set in to established state.
4.6.1  Format     LYNQ 
 Command                 Response

                         OK
 AT+CGACT=<state>,<cid
                         or
 >
                         ERROR
                         [+CGACT: <cid>,<state>]
                         [<CR><LF>+CGACT:<cid>,<state>
 AT+CGACT?
                         […]]
                         OK
                         +CGACT:  (list of supported <state>s)
 AT+CGACT=?
     CONFIDENTIALOK
 Max response time       45s, depend on network status

4.6.2  Field

 Parameters              Description


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    50

                         Integer type; indicates the activation state of PDP context activation
 <state>                 0: Deactivated
                         1: Activated
                         Integer type; specifies a particular PDP context definition. Only one
 <cid>                   <cid> can be activated or deactivated at the same time.
                         <cid> values of 1-15 are supported.

   4.8  AT+CEREG       - EPS   network   registration   status

   This command  controls the presentation of an unsolicited result code +CEREG: <stat> when <n>=1

   and there is a change in the UE's EPS network registration status, or result code +CEREG:

   <stat>[,<tac>,<ci>[,<AcT>]] when <n>=2 and there is a change of the network cell. The parameters

   <AcT>, <tac> and <ci> are provided only if available. The value <n>=3 further extends the unsolicited

   result code with [,<cause_type>,<reject_cause>], when available, when the value of <stat> changes.

   If the UE requests PSM for reducing its power consumption, the set command controls the

   presentation of an unsolicited result code:

   +CEREG:<n>,<stat>[,[<tac>],[<ci>],[<AcT>][,[<cause_type>],[<reject_cause>][,[<Active

   Time>],[<Periodic TAU>]]]]. When <n>=4, the unsolicited result code will provide the UE with

   additional information for the active time value and the extended periodic TAU value if there is a

   change of the network cell in E-UTRAN. The value <n>=5 further enhances the unsolicited result

   code with <cause_type> and <reject_cause> when the value of <stat> changes. The parameters

   <AcT>,<tac>,<ci>,<cause_type>,<reject_cause>,<Active Time> and <Periodic TAU> are provided
   only if available. LYNQ 

   The read command  returns the status of result code presentation and an integer <stat> which shows

   whether the network has currently indicated the registration of the MT. Location information elements

   <tac>, <ci> and <AcT> if available, are returned only when <n>=2 and MT is registered in the network.
The  parameters   [,<cause_type>,<reject_cause>], if available, are returned when <n>=3.

                         Bits
                         8 7 6
                         0 0 0 value is incremented in multiples of 10 minutes.
                         0 0 1 value is incremented in multiples of 1 hour.
                  LYNQ0   1 0 value is incremented in multiples of 10 hours.
                         0 1 1 value is incremented in multiples of 2 seconds.
                         1 0 0 value is incremented in multiples of 30 seconds.
                         1 0 1 value is incremented in multiples of 1 minute.
                         1 1 1 value indicates that the timer is deactivated.

  5.6  AT+CEDRXS        - Control  the  setting  of the  UE  eDRX    parameters

  The set command controls the setting of the UE’s eDRX parameters. The command controls whether

  the UE wants to apply eDRX or not, as well as the requested eDRX value for each specified type of

  access technology.

  The also controls the presentation of an unsolicited result code +CEDRXP:

  <AcTtype>[,<Requested_eDRX_value>[,<NW-provided_eDRX_value>[,<Paging_time_window>]]]

  when  <n>=2 and there is a change in the eDRX parameters provided by the network.

  A special form of the command can be given as +CEDRXS=3. In this form, eDRX will be disabled and

  data for all parameters in the command +CEDRXS will be removed or, if available, set to the
  manufacturer specificLYNQdefault values.     
  Note: not support this command.

  5.6.1  Format

   Command                 Response
AT+CEDRXS=<mode>[,<        OK
   AcT-type>[,<Requested_  If error is related to wrong AT syntax:

  is returned.

5.19.2   Field

 Parameters              Description

                         INTEGER  TYPE; SPECIFIES  DEVICE  CONTROL   OPTION.
                         0 Unbind cid for LWIP
                         1 Bind cid for LWIP once and no rebind if re-activate PDN
                         context with same cid after deactivation.
 <op>
                         2 Bind cid for LWIP and rebind if re-activate PDN context with
                         the same cid after deactivation.
                         3 Auto dial and bind cid when power on, saved in NVM after

                         Integer type; specifies a particular non secondary PDP context
 <cid>                   definition.
                         <cid> values of 1-15 are supported.
                         Integer type; specifies whether report URC +ECNETDEVCTL:<state>
 <urc_en>                0 Disable URC +ECNETDEVCTL:<state>
                  LYNQ1   Enable URC  +ECNETDEVCTL:<state> 
                         Integer type; specifies cid bound state.
 <state>                 0 Bind cid for LWIP failure
                         1 Bind cid for LWIP success
                         Note:
                         If the <cid> for PDN context defined but deactivated, bind this <cid> will
                         auto activate PDN context. If
                         the <cid> for PDN context undefined, bind this <cid> will cause
                         activation failed.

5.20CONFIDENTIALAT+CEMODE - UE modes   of operation   for  EPS

The set command is used to set the MT to operate according to the specified mode of operation for EPS,
see 3GPP TS 24.301 [83]. If the requested mode of operation is not supported, an ERROR or +CME
ERROR  response is returned. Extended error responses are enabled by the +CMEE command.
The read command returns the mode of operation set by the TE, independent of the current serving cell
capability and independent of the current serving cell Access Technology.
The test command is used for requesting information on the supported MT modes of operation as a


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    78
   EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

   compound value

   5.21   AT+CGCONTRDP          - PDP   context   read  dynamic    parameters

The  execution  command  returns the relevant information for an active non-secondary PDP context with
   the context identifier <cid>. If the MT has dual stack capabilities, at least one pair of lines with
   information is returned per <cid>. First one line with the IPv4 parameters followed by one line with the
   IPv6 parameters. If this MT with dual stack capabilities indicates more than more than two IP addresses
   of DNSCONFIDENTIALservers, multiple of such pairs of lines are returned.
   If the parameter <cid> is omitted, the relevant information for all active non secondary PDP contexts is
   returned.
   The test command returns a list of <cid>s associated with active non secondary contexts.


   Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    79

   8.3  AT+HTTPGET          Send   HTTP   GET   request    to HTTP(S)    server

   According to in the AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader"[,<request_header>], the AT+HTTPGET setting
   command  has two forms:
   If is 0, after sending AT+HTTPGET, if CONNECT is output within 20 seconds, it means the HTTP(S)
   server connection is successful, if not, it will output +HTTP ERROR:<err>. If <request_header> is 1, it
   will immediately output CONNECT   after sending AT+HTTPGET,   and  switch to transparent
transmission    mode,  and receive custom request headers. After input custom request headers, if
   CONNECT    is output within 20 seconds, it means successfully connect to HTTP server, if not, it will
   output +HTTP ERROR:<err>  .
   After CONFIDENTIALsend the AT+HTTPGET and get OK, it will output URC +HTTPGET:<err>,<httprspcode>
   [,content_length] after the data download is complete. If the HTTP response headers includes
   Content-Length, <content_length>will display.
   When  the GET  request is executed and the download is interrupted by network problems, it will
   display +HTTPGET:   <err>,<httprspcode>,<content_length>, the value of <err> is 11, and
   <content_length> indicates the length of data already download.  Use  AT+HTTPREAD    or
   AT+HTTPREADFILE     to read the downloaded  part. After the network is restored, use the
   AT+HTTPGETEX    command  to download the remaining part.


   Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    110
  EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

  8.3.1  Format

   Command                 Response

                           +HTTPGET:  (1-2048),(1-65535)
   AT+HTTPGET=?
                           OK
                           For <request_header> is 0:
                           OK
                           If connect success:
                           CONNECT
                           after get response:
                           +HTTPGET:  <err>,<httprspcode>[,<content_length>]

                           For <requestheader> is 1:
                           If parameter valid:
   AT+HTTPGET[=<data_le    CONNECT
   ngth>[,<input_time>]]   TA switch to transparent transmission mode, and receive customized
                           request headers:
                           OK
                           Success connect with HTTP server:
                           CONNECT
                           After receive the response:
                           +HTTPGET:  <err>,<httprspcode>[,<content_length>]
                    LYNQIf  input time is out of <input_time> and receive data less than
                           <data_length>, TA will turn to command mode and display:
                           +HTTP ERROR:<err>
   Max response time       5 s

8.5  AT+HTTPPOST         Send   POST    request   to HTTP(S)    Server

  This command is used to send HTTP POST request. After AT+HTTPPOST is input, TA will output
  CONNECT   and enter the transparent transmission mode. The user enters the information that the
  POST  request needs to carry through the UART port, and once the input length reaches the
  <data_length>, TA exit the transparent transmission mode, return OK, and begin connect. If
  CONNECT   is output within 20 seconds, it means the HTTP(S) server connection is successful; if
  not, +HTTP ERROR: <err> will be output.
  The following URC will output after the response are downloaded:
  +HTTPPOST:<err>,<httprspcode>[,<content_length>]. .
  According to the <request_header> in the AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader"[,<request_header>],
  the request data has two forms. If <request_header> is 0, the request data only need to include
  POST  request body, If <request_header> is 1, the request data need to include POST request
  headers and POST request body.

8.5.1  Format

 Command                 Response

                         +HTTPPOST:  (1-4096),(1-65535)
 AT+HTTPPOST=?    LYNQ 
                         OK

   
     CONFIDENTIAL


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    113
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                         If <request_header> is 0 (disable custom HTTP request headers)
                         CONNECT
                         TA switch to transparent transmission mode, and input request data:
                         HTTP POST  request body. When the data length reaches the
                         <data_length>,TA back to command mode and response:
                         OK
                         receive the HTTP(S) server’s response:
                         +HTTPPOST:<err>,<httprspcode>[,<content_length>]

                         If input time is out of <input_time> and receive data less than
                         <data_length>,TA will turn to command mode and response:
                         +HTTP ERROR:  <err>
                         If there is any error, response:
                         +HTTP ERROR:<err>
 AT+HTTPPOST=<data_l
 ength>[,<input_time>]   If <request_header> is 1 (enable custom HTTP request headers)

                         HTTP POST  request headers and HTTP POST request body. When the

                  LYNQOK

  8.6  AT+HTTPPOSTFILE            Send   POST    request   in file

  This command  can send the POST request that save in the file. According the <request_header> in
  the AT+HTTPCFG="requestheader"[,<request_header>], the command has two forms:
  If <request_header> is 0, the content of the file is POST content; if <request_header> is 1,the content
  of the file is POST request headers and POST content. After the server’s response is downloaded
  successfully or the download is interrupted due to network reasons, TA will send URC

<port>

       CONFIDENTIALWhether        to automatically set synchronized time to local UTC. Default is
   <autosync>              0.
                           0  Not set
                           1  Set
                           String type
                           Time synchronized from NTP server.
   <time>                  The format is: “yyyy/mm/dd, hh:mm:ss”, where characters indicate
                           year , month, day, hour, minutes and seconds, E.g. 6th of May 2014,
                           22:10:00 equals to "2014/05/06,22:10:00".

10        SSL        AT      Commands

10.1   AT+SSLCFG       - Configure   SSL   related  parameters

This command  is used to configure the cipher suite of the SSL context, CA certificate path,

authentication mode, client certificate path, client key and other SSL configuration. These parameters

will be used in the handshake process. If the third parameter is default, it means to query the current

configuration.

<SSL_ctxID> is the index of the SSL context. The module supports up to six SSL contexts. Multiple

SSL  connections can be established based on one SSL context.

   AT+MCONNECT=?
                           OK

   AT+MCONNECT=<clean      +MCONNECT:   <result>
   _session>,<keepalive>   or
                           ERROR
                           +MCONNECT:<clean_session>,<keepalive>
   AT+MCONNECT?
                    LYNQOK                     
   Max response time       30 s

  12.3.2   Field
Parameters                 Description
       CONFIDENTIAL


  Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    143
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                         This parameter specifies the handling of the Session state.
                         0  If CleanSession is set to 0, the Server must resume communications
                         with the Client based on state from the current Session (as identified by
                         the Client identifier). If there is no Session associated with the Client
                         identifier the Server must create a new Session. The Client and Server
                         MUST  store the Session after the Client and Server are disconnected.
                         After the disconnection of a Session that had CleanSession set to 0, the
 <clean_session>         Server MUST store further QoS 1 and QoS 2 messages that match any
                         subscriptions that the client had at the time of disconnection as part of
                         the Session state. It may also store QoS 0 messages that meet the
                         same criteria.
                         1  If CleanSession is set to 1, the Client and Server must discard any
                         previous Session and start a new one. This Session lasts as long as the
                         Network Connection. State data associated with this Session must not
                         be reused in any subsequent Session.

 <keepalive>             30-1800  The Keep Alive is a time interval measured in seconds.

                         The result of connecting mqtt server operation:
 <result>                SUCCESS
                         FAILURE

 st>                      This command will return HTTP response header fields and file path

                          fail, this command just return response header fields. Specially, the
                          HEAD  request only return response header fields. For POST, must set
                          Conten-Length header item and POST's content data.

                            <type>s)

   ,<type>,<serverIP>,<server +CIPOPEN: <err>,<link_num>
   Port>[,<localPort>]      Or
                            ERROR

  15.1.2  Field

   ParametersCONFIDENTIALDescription
                            Identifies a connection, from 0 to 5, and default value is 0.
   <link_num>               If AT+CIPMODE=<mode>   is set, the <link_num> is restricted to be
                            only 0, +++ is exit send and receive mode.
                            Identifies the type of transmission protocol.
   <type>                   TCP: Transfer Control Protocol
                            UDP: User Datagram Protocol


  Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    178
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                          Identifies the IP address of server.
 <serverIP>
                          If type is UDP serverIP set to empty, except AT+CIPMODE=1.
                          Identifies the port ofserver, from 0 to 65535, and default value is 0.
 <serverPort>
                          If type is UDP serverPort set to empty, except AT+CIPMODE=1
                          Identifies the port of local socket, from 0 to 65535, and default value is
 <localPort>
                          0.
                          Indicate the result of operation.
                          SUCCESS:  is success
                          FAIL: is failure
 <err>
                          if AT+CIPMODE=1:
                          CONNECT:   connect establish and can be send or receive data.
                          OK: exit online data send and receive mode.

15.2   AT+CIPSEND         Send   data  through   TCP   or  UDP   Connection

This command  is used to send data through TCP or UDP connection on non_transparent mode.

15.2.1  Format

 Command                  Response

                          +CIPSEND:   (list of supported <link_num>s), (list of supported
                          <length>s)
 AT+CIPSEND=?     LYNQ 
                          OK
                          OK
 AT+CIPSEND?              or
                          ERROR
   
     CONFIDENTIAL

EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                          OK

                          +CIPSEND:
                          <err>,<link_num>,<reqSendLength>,<cnfSendLength>
                          ERROR
 AT+CIPSEND=<link_num>
 ,[<length>]
                          This command is used to send data to remote side on non_transparent
                          mode.
 AT+CIPSEND=<link_num>
                          Single <Ctrl+Z>is start send.
 ,,,,<data>
                          Single <ESC> is used to cancel the sending.
                          Single<Ctrl+D>means exit the sending mode.
 AT+CIPSEND=<link_num>
                          <Ctrl+Z> is 0x1A, <ESC> is 0x1B, <Ctrl+D> is x04.
 ,[<length>],<serverIP>,<ser
                          These signals take effect when no length is specified
 verPort>
                          Note
 (This format is for UDP
                          1. In transparent mode <length> will be ignore or the <length> is
 connect)
                          empty, when input data length reach to 2500 will trigger send out.
                          2. Small data can be sent as follows:
                          TCP type: AT+CIPSEND=<link_num>,,,,<data>
                          UDP  type:
                          AT+CIPSEND=<link_num>,,<serverIP>,<serverPort>,<data>
 Max response time        30 s
15.2.2  Field     LYNQ 
 Parameters               Description

 <link_num>               Identifies a connection, from 0 to 5, and default value is 0.

                          Identifies the IP address of server.The IP address format consists of 4
                          octets,separated by decimal point: "AAA.BBB.CCC.DDD"

                          Identifies the port ofserver, from 0 to 65535, and default value is 0.
 <serverPort>CONFIDENTIAL
                          Identifies the contents for sending, and The maximum length is 512.
 <data>

                          A numeric parameter that requested number of data bytes to be
 <reqSendLength>
                          transmitted.


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    180
  EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                            A numeric parameter that confirmed number of data bytes to be

   <cnfSendLength>

                            Indicate the result of operation.

17.9   AT+CTM2MRMODE             Set  display   mode   of downlink    data

This command  set the display mode of the downlink data. When set to 1, the received downlink data
will be displayed immediately, such as +CTM2MRECV: <data>. When set to 2, UE will cache data
and display +CTM2MRECV,  the data can be read by AT+CTM2MREAD.  If there is cached data, UE
will not enter HibernateLYNQor Sleep2 mode. UE buffers up to 8 downlink data, after exceeding this limit,
the oldest data will be discard first. 1 is default value, and restore to 1 when reset.

17.9.1  Format

 Command                 Response

                         OK
 AT+CTM2MRMODE=<m
                         or
 ode>
                         +CTM2M  ERROR:<err>
                         +CTM2MRMODE:    <mode>
 AT+CTM2MRMODE?
     CONFIDENTIALOK

17.9.2  Field

 Parameters              Description


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    198
  EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                           Integer type
                           0---Received the downlink data, buffer it and no indication.
   <mode>                  1---Received the downlink data, immediately display +CTM2MRECV:
                           [<Length>,]<data>, 1 is default value
                           2---Received the downlink data, buffer it and display +CTM2MRECV

                         Config mode

                  LYNQAuto   send observe ack packet

                         Authertication

   Parameters              Description

                           Integer type;
   <time>                  The value is the max response time of Wi-Fi scan.
                           Default value is 12000 ms
                           Integer type;
   <maxbssidnum>           The value is the max required Wi-Fi SSID number.
                           Default value is 5
                           Integer type;
   <round>                 The value is max number of Wi-Fi scan rounds.
                           Default value is 1
                           Integer type; unit : second
   <scantimeout>    LYNQThe   value is max search time per Wi-Fi scan round.
                           Default value is 5
                           Integer type;
                           The value is the priority of Wi-Fi scan. [ 0 means data preferred; 1
   <priority>
                           means Wi-Fi preferred]
                           Default value is 0
                           Integer type;
<channelreclen>            The value is the max scan time on each frequency channel. [ unit is ms]
                           Default value is 280 ms
                           Integer type;
                           The count of the frequency channel to be scanned.
   <channelcount>
       CONFIDENTIALDefault       value is 1
                           If channelCount is 1 and channelId1 is 0, UE will scanning all frequency.


  Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    257
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                         Integer type;
                         The value is the specific frequency channel of Wi-Fi scan.
 <channelid1>            If channel ID is configured, the range of channel ID value is [1-14].
 ~                       [ If all channel ID value 0, means no channel specified, all frequency
 <channelid14>           channels will
                         be scanned]
                         Default value is 0
                         string type;
                         The string is a specific BSSID value(MAC address).
 <SPECIFIC  MAC          UE will only report the specific BSSID info if the BSSID scan
 ADDRESS>                successfully.
                         If failed to scan the specific BSSID, UE will report an empty scan result.
                         Default value is NULL
                         Note:
                         1> ECWIFISCAN  AT could be executed in CFUN0 / CFUN4 / NO-USIM
                         / Normal state.
                         2> Wi-Fi scan only could be executed while PHY in Idle state.
                         If the priority is set to 0 and UE in connection state, UE will not start the
                         Wi-Fi scan procedure until UE return back to Idle state.
                         If the priority is set to 1 and UE in connection state, UE will release
                         connection immediately and start Wi-Fi scan procedure.
                         3> <time> value should not be less than <round> multiplied by
                         <scantimeout>.
                         4> <maxbssidnum> means  the max required Wi-Fi SSID number.
                  LYNQCase    1 - If UE has detected the required Wi-Fi SSID, UE will stop Wi-Fi
                         procedure and report the detected Wi-Fi result.
                         Case 2 - If UE has not detected the required Wi-Fi SSID before execute
                         time out, UE will abort Wi-Fi procedure and report the detected Wi-Fi
                         result.
                         Case 3 - The round is set to 3 and maxbssidnum is set to 10, if in the
                         first round UE could detect five Wi-Fi SSID, in the second round UE
                         could detect three Wi-Fi SSID, and in the third round UE could detect six
                         Wi-Fi SSID. UE will delete the duplicate Wi-Fi SSID and sort the Wi-Fi
                         SSID by descending order, then report the ten Wi-Fi SSID result.

                         could detect three Wi-Fi SSID, and in the third round UE could detect six
                         Wi-Fi SSID. UE will delete the duplicate Wi-Fi SSID and sort the Wi-Fi
                         SSID by descending order, then report the ten Wi-Fi SSID result.
ExampleCONFIDENTIAL:

  25.7   AT+CBAUDLYNQUART       BAUD    rate setting 
  Specifies the data rate, in addition to 921600 bits/s or 9600 bits/s, at which the DCE will accept

  commands.  May be used to select operation at rates at which the DCE is not capable of automatically

  detecting the data rate being used by the DTE.

   Test Command            Response
AT+ CBAUD  =?              +CBAUD:(list of supported<rate>s)
                           OK
   Execution Command       Response
   AT+CBAUD=<rate>CONFIDENTIALOK
   Read Command            Response
   AT+CBAUD?               +BAUDRATE:<rate>

                           OK
   Max response time       300 ms

  Parameters are defined below:


  Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    270
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

 Parameters              Description
 <rate>                  The rate, in bits per second, at which the DTE-DCE interface should
                         operate. Currently, the following rates are supported:
                         600,1200,2400,4800,9600,19200,38400,57600,115200,230400,46080
                         0,921600.If unspecified, Default rate is 115200 bps.

   Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    282

 <mcc>                   Indicate the mobile country code.

 <mnc>                   Indicate the mobile network code.

 <mnc_len>               The length of mnc.

26.10    AT+QCCID      Read   the  USIM   ICCID   field

This command  is used to Read the USIM ICCID field.

                                   Response

 AT+QCCID
                                   OK
 Max response time                 300 ms

Parameters are defined below:

 Parameter note:  LYNQDescription            
                         International Circuit Card (ICC) ID of the (U)SIM. Formatted as a
 <ICCID>                 numeric string without double quotes. The string can be up to a
                         maximum  of 20 digits long.

26.11    AT$QCSIMCFG           Set  sim  configuration
   
This command  is used to set sim configuration.

                                   Response
     CONFIDENTIAL("SimSimulator","SimPowerSave","SimPresenceDetect","Si
 Test Command
                                   mSlot","SoftSim")
 AT$QCSIMCFG=?

                                   OK


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    283
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                                   Response
                                   $QCSIMCFG:   "SimSimulator",0
                                   $QCSIMCFG:   "SimPowerSave",1
 Read Command                      $QCSIMCFG:   "SimPresenceDetect",1
 AT$QCSIMCFG?                      $QCSIMCFG:   "SimSlot",0
                                   $QCSIMCFG:   "SoftSim",0

                                   OK
                                   Response
 Set Command                       OK
 AT$QCSIMCFG=<param>,<value>       or
                                   ERROR
 Max response time                 300 ms

Parameters are defined below:


 Parameter note:         Description

                         Enable/disable virtual SIM card for instrument test,such as CMW500.
                         Note:
                         a) Supported values: (0,1)
 “SimSimulator”
                         b) Default value: 0
                         c) Set it, then virtual SIM or real SIM take effect by terminal reset or
                  LYNQentering  AT+CFUN=0/1. 
                         Enable/disable SIM power save.
                         Note:
 “SimPowerSave”
                         a) Supported values: (0,1)
                         b) Default value: 1.
                         Enable/disable SIM presence detection
                         Note:
 “SimPresenceDetect”
                         a) Supported values: (0,1)
                         b) Default value: 1.
                         Select SIM slot, only support SIM slot 0 or 1. The
                         hardware interface of SIM shall be ready if select
     CONFIDENTIALSIM         slot 0 or 1.
 “SimSlot”               Note:
                         a) Supported values: (0,1)
                         b) Default value: 0
                         c) It must be restricted to execute in power off (AT+CFUN=0) state.


Copyright© Shanghai Mobiletek Communication Ltd.                                    284
EC_CAT1_ AT_Command_User_Guide

                         Enable/disable SoftSIM if integrated, then start
                         SoftSIM or physical SIM card when power on.
                         Note:
 “SoftSIM”               a) Supported values: (0,1)
                         b) Default value: 0
                         c) It must be restricted to execute in power off (AT+CFUN=0) state.
                         d) Shall integrate SoftSIM lib to EC SDK with SoftSIM vender.

26.12    AT+CNUM       request   subscriber    number

Action command returns the MSISDNs (up to 4) related to the subscriber stored in the USIM.

 Test Command                      Response
 AT+CNUM=?                         OK

                                   Response
                                   [+CNUM:  [<alpha>],<number>,<type>]
                                   [+CNUM:  [<alpha>],<number>,<type>]
 Set Command                       […]
 AT+CNUM
                                   OK
                                   If there is any error, response:
                                   +CME  ERROR:  <err>
 Max response time LYNQ300             ms    

Parameters are defined below:


 Parameter note:         Description

                         STRING TYPE
 <ALPHA>
                         Optional alphanumeric string associated with <number>.
                         String type
 <NUMBER>
                         Phone number of format specified by <type>
     CONFIDENTIALType        of address octet in integer format (refer 3GPP TS24.008
                         subclause 10.5.4.7), e.g.
 <TYPE>                  129 Unknown type
                         145 International type (use “+” for international access code)
                         161 National type
